{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Cf114e8sc4iCC2KEEke5P","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The Cahn-Hilliard benchmark is now OpenMP-parallel and reaches **~7,800 MCellUpdates/s vs 72 single-threaded** (≈108× on this 2-socket, 128-core EPYC) with bit-identical results at any thread count.\n\nWhat I changed, all within the two existing files:\n\n**`cahn_hilliard.cpp`**\n- Added `#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)` to the three grid sweeps: `computeChemicalPotential`, `cahnHilliardUpdate`, and `initializeConcentration`. Each cell writes only its own index from read-only inputs, so no synchronization is needed, and the pseudo-random initializer is index-based, so parallelizing it preserves exact values. Parallelizing initialization also gives correct NUMA first-touch placement, since the same static schedule is used in the compute loops.\n- Added a `bindThreads()` routine called at startup. This turned out to be the dominant factor on this machine: with the runtime's default (no thread binding), threads migrate across the two NUMA nodes and performance capped at ~370 MCU/s; with binding it's ~7,800. It reads the core topology from sysfs, groups CPUs into physical cores, and pins threads equivalently to `OMP_PROC_BIND=spread OMP_PLACES=cores` (consecutive threads share a core, so adjacent stencil chunks share its caches). It's skipped entirely if the user sets `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES` themselves, and falls back gracefully if sysfs is unavailable. Setting the env vars in `main` doesn't work because libgomp parses them in an ELF constructor before `main`, hence the explicit `sched_setaffinity` approach (glibc only, no new dependencies).\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`** — `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and link `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`; parallelization is unconditional.\n\nCorrectness checks: output hashes (`-r`) are identical between 1 thread and 256 threads, across non-cubic odd grid sizes (100×64×37), the default 64³ grid, and with user-supplied binding env vars; validation passes in all cases."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":567,"cache_read_input_tokens":34208,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":567},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"11b997c7-bb10-42cc-a258-47736e22e15e","uuid":"f2a86ed7-b911-4b2d-a200-9caff008c1a5","timestamp":"2026-09-13T06:56:26.922Z","request_id":"req_011Cf114d2dyGfmyDnC1vQqh"}
